- **cis-eqtl-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a list of predicted enhancer–gene (gene–peak) pairs must be validated against an independent cis-eQTL reference set and summarized with AUROC/AUPRC benchmark metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cis-eqtl-benchmark-evaluation))
